Audit order: 192

Requirement ID: I2-026
Source family: I2
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: UR I2 Structural Requirements for Polar Class Ships
Source revision: Rev.4 Dec 2019
Clause / section / page: I2.5.3 / Framing - General / 10
Source URL: https://iacs.org.uk/resolutions/unified-requirements/ur-i/ur-i2-rev4-cln
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/ur-i2rev4.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 2142453d2965997a772f451982278e622413273048097924173216bd278df9a7
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-I2-026-20260915T094555989036Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The strength of a framing member is dependent upon the fixity that is provided at its supports. Fixity can be assumed where framing members are either continuous through the support or attached to a supporting section with a connection bracket. In other cases, simple support is to be assumed unless the connection can be demonstrated to provide significant rotational restraint. Fixity is to be ensured at the support of any framing which terminates within an ice-strengthened area.
Normalized requirement:

Fixity is to be ensured at the support of any framing which terminates within an ice-strengthened area.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-026-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-026-StructuralMemberShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-026-StructuralMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:continuousThroughSupport ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each structural member must state whether it is continuous through its support."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:terminatesWithinIceStrengthenedArea ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each structural member must state whether it terminates within the ice-strengthened area."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameSupportCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:fixedFrameSupport
            nltl:simpleFrameSupport
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each structural member must specify either fixed frame support or simple frame support."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasMemberSupport ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-026-MemberSupportShape> ;
        sh:message "Each structural member must identify its member support."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:significantRotationalRestraintEvidence ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact ;
        sh:message "Significant rotational restraint evidence must be an evidence artifact."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The support condition must be fixed for continuous members, members with a connection bracket, members with demonstrated significant rotational restraint, and members terminating within an ice-strengthened area; otherwise it must be simple." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:hasMemberSupport ?support .
  $this nltl:frameSupportCondition ?condition .
  FILTER (
    (
      ?condition = nltl:fixedFrameSupport &&
      NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:continuousThroughSupport ?continuousValue .
        FILTER(?continuousValue = true)
      } &&
      NOT EXISTS {
        ?support nltl:connectionBracketPresent ?bracketValue .
        FILTER(?bracketValue = true)
      } &&
      NOT EXISTS {
        ?support nltl:demonstratedRotationalRestraint ?restraintValue .
        FILTER(?restraintValue = true)
      } &&
      NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:terminatesWithinIceStrengthenedArea ?terminationValue .
        FILTER(?terminationValue = true)
      }
    )
    ||
    (
      ?condition = nltl:simpleFrameSupport &&
      (
        EXISTS {
          $this nltl:continuousThroughSupport ?continuousValue .
          FILTER(?continuousValue = true)
        } ||
        EXISTS {
          ?support nltl:connectionBracketPresent ?bracketValue .
          FILTER(?bracketValue = true)
        } ||
        EXISTS {
          ?support nltl:demonstratedRotationalRestraint ?restraintValue .
          FILTER(?restraintValue = true)
        }
      )
    )
    ||
    (
      ?condition != nltl:fixedFrameSupport &&
      ?condition != nltl:simpleFrameSupport
    )
    ||
    (
      EXISTS {
        $this nltl:terminatesWithinIceStrengthenedArea ?terminationValue .
        FILTER(?terminationValue = true)
      } &&
      ?condition != nltl:fixedFrameSupport
    )
  )
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Evidence is required on the structural member when significant rotational restraint is demonstrated by an associated member support." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:hasMemberSupport ?support .
  ?support nltl:demonstratedRotationalRestraint ?restraintValue .
  FILTER(?restraintValue = true)
  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    $this nltl:significantRotationalRestraintEvidence ?evidence .
  }
}
"""
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-026-MemberSupportShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectionBracketPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each member support must state whether a connection bracket is present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:demonstratedRotationalRestraint ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each member support must state whether significant rotational restraint is demonstrated."
    ] .

```

```turtle
    sh:message "The framing member support condition must be fixed when continuity, a connection bracket, or demonstrated rotational restraint establishes fixity; otherwise it must be simple support." .
```

The ice load transferred to a web frame from an ice stringer or from longitudinal framing shall be F = f_12*p*h*S [MN] (4.15). When calculating c_a, use l_a = 2*S. If p*h is less than 0.15 MN/m, use 0.15 MN/m. Use f_12 = 1.8.

```turtle
gs:TRF_057_WebFrameIceLoadShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;

        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]

        sh:path nltl:iceLoadHeight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameSpacing ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "webFrameIceLoad must equal 1.8 times webFrameSpacing times the greater of icePressure multiplied by iceLoadHeight and 0.15 MN/m, with a 1e-6 relative tolerance and a small absolute fallback." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:iceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameSpacing ?spacingNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameIceLoad ?loadNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?pressureValue .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?heightValue .
                ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?spacingValue .
                ?loadNode qudt:numericValue ?loadValue .

                BIND(
                    1800 * ?spacingValue *
                    IF(
                        (?pressureValue * ?heightValue) <
                        "0.15"^^xsd:decimal,
                        "0.15"^^xsd:decimal,
                        (?pressureValue * ?heightValue)
                    )
                    AS ?expectedLoad
                )

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?loadValue - ?expectedLoad) >
                    (
                        (ABS(?expectedLoad) * "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal) +
                        "0.000001"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:iceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameSpacing ?spacingNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameIceLoad ?loadNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?pVal .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?hVal .
                ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?sVal .
                ?loadNode qudt:numericValue ?reported .

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?pVal) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?hVal) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?sVal) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?reported) = xsd:decimal
                )

                BIND((?pVal * ?hVal) AS ?pressureHeight)
                BIND(IF(?pressureHeight < 0.15, 0.15, ?pressureHeight) AS ?effectivePressureHeight)
                BIND((1.8 * ?effectivePressureHeight * ?sVal * 1000) AS ?expected)

                FILTER (
                    ABS(?reported - ?expected) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expected) + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF057WebFrameIceLoadShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:webFrame ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:icePressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MegaPA
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadHeight ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameSpacing ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:webFrameIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "webFrameIceLoad must equal 1.8 times the effective ice pressure-height line load times webFrameSpacing; values below 0.15 MN/m use 0.15 MN/m, with a 1e-6 relative tolerance." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                      nltl:iceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameSpacing ?spacingNode ;
                      nltl:webFrameIceLoad ?loadNode .
                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?spacingNode qudt:numericValue ?s .
                ?loadNode qudt:numericValue ?f .
                BIND((?p * ?h) AS ?lineLoad)
                BIND(IF(?lineLoad < 0.15, 0.15, ?lineLoad) AS ?effectiveLineLoad)
                BIND((1800 * ?effectiveLineLoad * ?s) AS ?expectedLoad)
                FILTER(
                    ABS(?f - ?expectedLoad) >
                    IF(
                        ABS(?expectedLoad) < 0.000001,
                        0.000001,
                        ABS(?expectedLoad) * 0.000001
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-011-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDailyLowTemperatureObservation ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:dailyLowTemperatureObservation ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:dailyTemperatureObservationDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each daily low-temperature observation must have one calendar date."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:dailyLowTemperature ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Each daily low-temperature observation must carry one daily low-temperature quantity in degrees Celsius."
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:dataPeriod ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:YR ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide one data period quantity in years."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:meanDailyLowTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The externally calculated mean daily low temperature must be provided in degrees Celsius."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the data period is shorter than 10 years, the administration dataset approval status must be Approved." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:dataPeriod ?period .
                ?period qudt:numericValue ?years ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/YR> .
                FILTER (?years < 10)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:administrationDatasetApprovalStatus
                          nltl:evidenceStateApproved .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A short data period must not have an administration dataset approval status other than Approved." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:dataPeriod ?period ;
                      nltl:administrationDatasetApprovalStatus ?status .
                ?period qudt:numericValue ?years ;
                        qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/YR> .
                FILTER (?years < 10)
                FILTER (?status != nltl:evidenceStateApproved)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Ice-strengthened ships constructed on or after 1 January 2026 shall have either two independent echo-sounding devices or one echo-sounding device with two separate independent transducers. Other devices capable of depth sounding, such as fish finders, acceptable to the Administration, may be used as equivalent means of meeting this requirement.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_ice_strengthened = true AND construction_date >= '2026-01-01', THEN independent_echo_sounding_device_count >= 2 OR (echo_sounding_device_count >= 1 AND independent_transducer_count >= 2) OR administration_approved_equivalent_depth_sounding_device_present = true.
Required inputs: Ice-strengthening status, construction date, equipment topology, and equivalent approval

```turtle
            sh:sparql [
                sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipIceStrengthened ?ice ;
          nltl:constructionDate ?date .
    FILTER (
        ?ice = true &&
        (
            YEAR(?date) < 2026 ||
            (YEAR(?date) = 2026 && MONTH(?date) < 1) ||
            (YEAR(?date) = 2026 && MONTH(?date) = 1 && DAY(?date) < 1)
        )
    )
}
"""
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                        sh:minInclusive "2026-01-01"^^xsd:date ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ]
                ]
                [

                                        sh:minCount 1
                                    ]

                            )

                                sh:minCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    )
                ]
            )
        )
    ) ;
    sh:message "For an ice-strengthened ship constructed on or after 2026-01-01, provide two independent echo-sounding devices, or one device with two independent transducers, or an Administration-approved equivalent depth-sounding device." .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF088ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumEngineTorqueKnown ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall state whether maximum engine torque is known."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF088KnownShape
        gen:TRF088UnknownShape
    ) .

gen:TRF088KnownShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumEngineTorqueKnown ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] .

gen:TRF088UnknownShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumEngineTorqueKnown ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumEngineTorque ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF088TorqueQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "When maximum engine torque is unknown, the ship shall record the torque value selected from TRAFICOM Table 6-10."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF088TableLookupCaseShape ;
        sh:message "When maximum engine torque is unknown, a Table 6-10 lookup case shall be recorded."
    ] .

gen:TRF088TorqueQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:KiloN-M
    ] .

gen:TRF088TableLookupCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableLookupApplied ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash10
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lookupSelectionEvidence ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltl:evidenceArtifact
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-007-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "Assessment date must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "Construction stage date must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:message "Delivery date must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an IA Super or IA ship constructed before 1 September 2003, the ship must have a permitted 2017 compliance method and the assessment date must be no later than 1 January of the year twenty years after delivery." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .

                FILTER (
                    ?iceClass IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa
                    )
                )

                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?constructionDate) < 2003
                    ||
                    (
                        YEAR(?constructionDate) = 2003
                        &&
                        (
                            MONTH(?constructionDate) < 9
                            ||
                            (
                                MONTH(?constructionDate) = 9
                                &&
                                DAY(?constructionDate) < 1
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate ;
                          nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate ;
                          nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ?method .

                    FILTER (
                        ?method IN (
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method,
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                        )
                    )

                    FILTER (
                        YEAR(?assessmentDate) < YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                        ||
                        (
                            YEAR(?assessmentDate) = YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                            &&
                            (
                                MONTH(?assessmentDate) < 1
                                ||
                                (
                                    MONTH(?assessmentDate) = 1
                                    &&
                                    DAY(?assessmentDate) <= 1
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:TRF_007_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have exactly one valid Finnish-Swedish ice class."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Any construction stage date shall be an xsd:date and shall not be repeated."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Any delivery date shall be an xsd:date and shall not be repeated."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Any assessment date shall be an xsd:date and shall not be repeated."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethodValue ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Any engine-output compliance method shall be one IRI-valued method resource."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Ships of ice class IA Super or IA shall provide a valid construction stage date so that applicability of the 2003 cutoff can be determined." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ic .
                FILTER (?ic IN (nltl:iceClassIaSuper, nltl:iceClassIa))
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?cs .
                    FILTER (datatype(?cs) = xsd:date)
                }
            }
        """ ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an IA Super or IA ship whose keel was laid or construction had reached a similar stage before 1 September 2003, the required 2017 compliance method, delivery date, assessment date, and deadline compliance shall be represented." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?ic .
                FILTER (?ic IN (nltl:iceClassIaSuper, nltl:iceClassIa))

                $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?cs .
                FILTER (datatype(?cs) = xsd:date)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?cs) < 2003 ||
                    (YEAR(?cs) = 2003 && MONTH(?cs) < 9)
                )

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?del .
                        FILTER (datatype(?del) = xsd:date)
                    } ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:assessmentDate ?ass .
                        FILTER (datatype(?ass) = xsd:date)
                    } ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ?method .
                        FILTER (?method IN (
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method,
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                        ))
                    } ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?del2 ;
                              nltl:assessmentDate ?ass2 ;
                              nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ?method2 .
                        FILTER (datatype(?del2) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (datatype(?ass2) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (?method2 IN (
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method,
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                        ))
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?ass2) < YEAR(?del2) + 20 ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?ass2) = YEAR(?del2) + 20 &&
                                MONTH(?ass2) = 1 &&
                                DAY(?ass2) = 1
                            )
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        """ ;
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-007-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An ice class is required to determine whether the conditional requirement applies."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an IA or IA Super ship whose construction stage predates 1 September 2003, the applicable 2017 section method, delivery date, and assessment date must show compliance by 1 January of the year twenty years after delivery." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass .
                FILTER (
                    ?iceClass IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIa,
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                    )
                )
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?anyConstructionDate .
                        FILTER (datatype(?anyConstructionDate) = xsd:date)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?earlyConstructionDate .
                        FILTER (datatype(?earlyConstructionDate) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (?earlyConstructionDate < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date)
                    }
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate ;
                          nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate ;
                          nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate ;
                          nltl:engineOutputComplianceMethod ?method .

                    FILTER (datatype(?constructionDate) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER (datatype(?deliveryDate) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER (datatype(?assessmentDate) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER (?constructionDate < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date)

                    FILTER (
                        YEAR(?assessmentDate) < YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                        ||
                        (
                            YEAR(?assessmentDate) = YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                            &&
                            MONTH(?assessmentDate) = 1
                            &&
                            DAY(?assessmentDate) <= 1
                        )
                    )

                    FILTER (
                        ?method IN (
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point2Method,
                            nltl:traficom2017Section3Point2Point4Method
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
gen:TRF078ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:tableLookupCase ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide exactly one linked lookup case for each of Tables 6-6 and 6-7."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:qualifiedMinCount 1 ;
        sh:qualifiedMaxCount 1
    ] ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "iceLoadCycleCount must equal k1 multiplied by k2, k3, N_class, and n_n." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?classCase ;
                      nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?locationCase ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2 ?k2Node ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK3 ?k3Node ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeLoadSpectrumCountFactor ?nnNode ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ?reported .

                ?classCase nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash6 ;
                            nltl:selectedIceClassCycleCount ?nclassNode .
                ?locationCase nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash7 ;
                               nltl:selectedPropellerLocationFactor ?k1Node .

                ?k2Node qudt:numericValue ?k2 .
                ?k3Node qudt:numericValue ?k3 .
                ?nnNode qudt:numericValue ?nn .
                ?nclassNode qudt:numericValue ?nclass .
                ?k1Node qudt:numericValue ?k1 .

                BIND ((?k1 * ?k2 * ?k3 * ?nclass * ?nn) AS ?expected)
                FILTER (xsd:decimal(?reported) != ?expected)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceClassIa
                    nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                    nltl:iceClassIb
                    nltl:iceClassIc
                    nltl:iceClassIi
                    nltl:iceClassIii
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:selectedIceClassCycleCount ;
                sh:node nltl:TRF078QuantityValueShape ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:tableReference ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash7
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:tableLookupPropellerLocation ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:propellerLocationValue ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:selectedPropellerLocationFactor ;
                sh:node nltl:TRF078QuantityValueShape ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) .

nltl:TRF078ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasTableLookupCase ;
        sh:node nltl:TRF078TableLookupCaseShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2 ;
        sh:node nltl:TRF078QuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK3 ;
        sh:node nltl:TRF078QuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadSpectrumCountFactor ;
        sh:node nltl:TRF078QuantityValueShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Both Table 6-6 and Table 6-7 lookup cases are required." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case6 .
                        ?case6 nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash6 .
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case7 .
                        ?case7 nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash7 .
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "iceLoadCycleCount must equal k1 multiplied by k2, k3, N_class, and n_n within the permitted numerical tolerance." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case6 ;
                      nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case7 ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2/qudt:numericValue ?k2 ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK3/qudt:numericValue ?k3 ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeLoadSpectrumCountFactor/qudt:numericValue ?nn ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ?reported .
                ?case6 nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash6 ;
                        nltl:selectedIceClassCycleCount/qudt:numericValue ?nclass .
                ?case7 nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash7 ;
                        nltl:selectedPropellerLocationFactor/qudt:numericValue ?k1 .
                BIND ((?k1 * ?k2 * ?k3 * ?nclass * ?nn) AS ?expected)
                FILTER (
                    ABS(xsd:decimal(?reported) - ?expected)
                    >
                    (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001 + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
                      nltl:hasTableLookupCase ?case7 ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK2 ?k2Node ;
                      nltl:iceLoadCycleCoefficientK3 ?k3Node ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeLoadSpectrumCountFactor ?nnNode ;

                        nltl:selectedIceClassCycleCount ?nclassNode .
                ?case7 nltl:tableReference nltl:traficomTable6Dash7 ;

                ?k2Node qudt:numericValue ?k2 .
                ?k3Node qudt:numericValue ?k3 .
                ?nclassNode qudt:numericValue ?nclass .
                ?nnNode qudt:numericValue ?nn .

                FILTER (
                    ABS(xsd:decimal(?actual) - ?expected) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expected) + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
```

```turtle
gen:I2_022_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;

    sh:property [

    ] ;
    sh:property [

    ] ;
    sh:property [

    ] ;
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-022-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPlating ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:plating ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-022-PlatingShape>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Applicable plating requires all declared calculation operands on their authoritative owners with the specified quantity value types and units." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:hasPlating ?plating .
    ?plating nltl:framingAngleOmega ?angleQuantity .
    ?angleQuantity qudt:numericValue ?angle .
    ?plating nltl:platingHullAreaClassification ?area .
    FILTER (datatype(?angle) = xsd:decimal)
    FILTER (?angle >= 70)
    FILTER (?area IN (
        nltl:bowIntermediateBottomHullArea,
        nltl:midbodyBottomHullArea,
        nltl:sternBottomHullArea
    ))
    FILTER (
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:averageIcePressure ?averagePressure .
            ?averagePressure a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?averagePressureValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
            FILTER (datatype(?averagePressureValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:peakPressureFactor ?peakFactor .
            ?peakFactor a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?peakFactorValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
            FILTER (datatype(?peakFactorValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:yieldStrength ?yield .
            ?yield a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?yieldValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> .
            FILTER (datatype(?yieldValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:loadPatchHeight ?patchHeight .
            ?patchHeight a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?patchHeightValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
            FILTER (datatype(?patchHeightValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            ?plating nltl:platingSupportedByStructuralMember ?member .
            ?member nltl:frameSpacing ?spacing .
            ?spacing a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?spacingValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
            FILTER (datatype(?spacingValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            ?plating nltl:platingSupportedByStructuralMember ?member2 .
            ?member2 nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ?selectedFactor .
            ?selectedFactor a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                qudt:numericValue ?selectedFactorValue ;
                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
            FILTER (datatype(?selectedFactorValue) = xsd:decimal)
        }
    )
}
"""
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-022-PlatingShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:plating ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingAngleOmega ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:sparql [
                a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
                sh:message "Non-applicable plating must have a framing angle below 70 degrees." ;
                sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:framingAngleOmega ?quantity .
    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value .
    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
    FILTER (?value < 70)
}
"""
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
                sh:message "Applicable plating must have a framing angle of at least 70 degrees." ;
                sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:framingAngleOmega ?quantity .
    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value .
    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
    FILTER (?value >= 70)
}
"""
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:platingHullAreaClassification ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:bowIntermediateBottomHullArea
                    nltl:midbodyBottomHullArea
                    nltl:sternBottomHullArea
                )
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:platingSupportedByStructuralMember ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:frameSpacing ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:selectedHullAreaFactor ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
```
